{D2589}]@##/_/rgya gar skad du/_ga ga nA ma la su pa ri shud+d+ho d+har+m+ma d+hA tu dz+nyA na gar+b+ha nA ma/_bod skad du/_nam mkha' dri ma med pa shin tu yongs su dag pa chos kyi dbyings kyi ye shes kyi snying po zhes bya ba/_dkyil 'khor 
[\[156a.6\]]thams cad bsdus par gyur pa/_bcom ldan 'das rje btsun 'jam dpal gyi don thams cad sgrub pa nam mkha' dri ma med pa shin tu yongs su dag pa chos kyi dbyings kyi ye shes kyi snying po zhes bya ba'i dkyil 'khor chen po'i don gyi dgongs pa sbas pa phyin ci ma log pa'i man ngag las brgyud pa 
[\[156a.7\]]brjod par bya ste/_de nas yang dag bshad bgyi ba/_/dkyil 'khor chen po dam pa ste/_/gru bzhir bgyi zhing sgo bzhi dang /_/rta babs bzhis ni rnam par brgyan/_/thig bzhi dang ni ldan gyur pa/_/dar dang me tog phreng bas brgyan/_/grwa yi phyogs ni thams cad dang /_/sgo 
[\[156b\]]
[\[156b.1\]]khyud kyi ni mtshams rnams su/_/rdo rje rin chen spras par ni/_/dkyil 'khor phyi rol thig gdab po/_/de yi rim pa'i nang bzhugs la/_/'khor lo 'dra bar rdo rje yi/_/thig gis yongs su bskor bar bya/_/ka ba brgyad kyis mdzes bgyis par/_/rdo rje ka ba'i nang logs su/_/
[\[156b.2\]]zla ba'i dkyil 'khor lngas brgyan la/_/dbus kyi dkyil 'khor dbus su ni/_/sna tshogs pad+ma yid 'ong ba/_/'dab ma brgyad pa ge sar bcas/_/dag pa thams cad rab tu gnas/_/de yi lte ba'i dbus su ni/_/'od bzang ston ka'i zla ba'i 'od/_/nyi ma 'char ka'i dkyil 
[\[156b.3\]]'khor 'dra/_/sen mo'i 'od ni shas cher dmar/_/chags bral la sogs chags pa che/_/kha dog sna tshogs 'bar ba'i 'od/_/skra mchog chen po mthon ka'i rtse/_/sangs rgyas rin chen cod pan ldan/_/zhal bzhi pa la phyag brgyad pa/_/sgeg pa la sogs nyams dang ldan/_/
[\[156b.4\]]sna tshogs na bza' yang dag bsnams/_/byis pa'i rgyan gyis yang dag spung /_/mtshan ni sum cu rtsa gnyis dang /_/dpe byad brgyad cus yang dag brgyan/_/chos kyi 'khor lo'i phyag rgya ches/_/phyag gnyis dag ni rnam par brgyan/_/ral gri mda' dang rdo rje yis/_/
[\[156b.5\]]g.yas pa'i phyag mchog rnam par mdzes/_/shes rab pha rol phyin dang gzhu/_/rdo rje dril mchog g.yon pa na/_/dri med dpe bral bsam mi khyab/_/shes rab ye shes dri ma med/_/mgon po sangs rgyas kun gyi dngos/_/dkyil 'khor mdun gyi gtso gyur la/_/
[\[156b.6\]]rdzogs sangs rdo rje'i skyil krung bcas/_/'jam pa'i dbyangs ni nges par dgod/_/pad+ma'i 'dab ma thams cad la/_/seng ge'i gdan gyi stengs bzhugs shing /_/thams cad rin chen bdun bskor bar/_/'khor lo can ni brgyad bri'o/_/gtsug tor chen po yi ge bdag_/shar gyi 'dab mar yang dag 
[\[156b.7\]]bri/_/gtsug tor gdugs ni dkar po dag_/nub kyi 'dab mar dgod par bya/_/gzi brjid phung po lho phyogs su/_/gtsug tor rnam rgyal byang phyogs su/_/rnam par 'thor ba dbang ldan phyogs/_/me yi phyogs su gyen 'byung dgod/_/gyen 'byung chen po bden bral phyogs/_/rgyal 
[\[157a\]]
[\[157a.1\]]ba rlung gi phyogs su dgod/_/sngags rnams kun la'ang sgrub pa po/_/'khor lo can ni de dag bri/_/rdo rje shugs kyis song nas su/_/dkyil 'khor bzhi po dag tu ni/_/mi bskyod pa la sogs pa bzhi/_/sangs rgyas thams cad dgod par bya/_/rdo rje sems dpa' la sogs 
[\[157a.2\]]pa'ang /_/sna tshogs rgyan dang gos 'chang ba/_/dbang ldan phyogs nas brtsams nas ni/_/[{]go rims [,]go rim [}]ji bzhin bri bar bya/_/de nyid kyi ni mtshams kyi char/_/spyan dang de bzhin mA ma kI/_/gos dkar de bzhin sgrol ma ni/_/rab tu 'bad de bri bar bya/_/sgo rnams su yang sgo srung ni/_/rang gi mtshan mas phyag brgyan pa/_/rdo 
[\[157a.3\]]rje slob dpon blo ldan pas/_/rab tu 'bad de bri bar bya/_/rdo rje shugs kyis byung nas ni/_/dkyil 'khor mchog ni gnyis pa ru/_/zur bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa/_/sgo srung rnams dang yang dag ldan/_/dag pa'i gzugs kyi dkyil 'khor du/_/der ni cho ga bzhin bri bya/_/shar phyogs su ni sa rnams bri/_/de 
[\[157a.4\]]bzhin lho ni rab 'bar ba'i/_/pha rol phyin pa thams cad bri/_/nub tu dbang rnams bcu po ste/_/byang du gzungs ni thams cad bri/_/de bzhin mtshams su sgeg mo sogs/_/dngos grub bde ba kun sogs pa/_/gsang sngags don dang ldan par bri/_/rdo rje shugs kyis byung nas su/_/dkyil 'khor 
[\[157a.5\]]dam pa gsum par ni/_/bskal pa bzang po thams cad dang /_/de bzhin sgo srung rnams kyang bri/_/rdo rje me tog la sogs pa'ang /_/[{]go rims [,]go rim [}]ji bzhin bri bar bya/_/de yi pho brang phyi rol bri/_/phyi rol rdo rje rigs kyi gnas/_/zlum zhing kun du bzang ba dang /_/sgo bzhi shin tu mdzes par yang /_/der ni 
[\[157a.6\]]tshangs pa la sogs pa/_/chung ma la sogs tshogs kyis brgyan/_/rgyan dang rang gi mtshan mar ldan/_/kun du rim bzhin legs par bri/_/phyi sgo'i shar gyi cha la ni/_/gshin rje gshed po'i phyag rgya che/_/thams cad bsrungs la rab brtson pa/_/rnam pa kun du legs par gzhag_/g.yon 
[\[157a.7\]]brkyang bas ni legs bzhugs shing /_/phyag g.yas dbyug pa 'phyar mdzad la/_/phyag g.yon thugs kar sdigs mdzub bsgreng /_/rab tu drag por bri bar bya/_/gzhan gyis mi thub khro bo che/_/phyag rgya chen po lho phyogs su/_/khro bo chen po rta mgrin gyi/_/phyag rgya chen po nub phyogs su/_/rdo 
[\[157b\]]
[\[157b.1\]]rje 'jigs byed spyan gyis ni/_/phyag rgya chen po byang phyogs su/_/phyi yi dkyil 'khor phyi rol du/_/bsrung ba'i phyir ni thams cad dgod/_/de yi phyi rol khor yug dang /_/khor yug chen po de bzhin bri/_/blo ldan yang dag kun rdzogs par/_/sngags zhes pas ni legs par bri/_/da ni sku mdog la 
[\[157b.2\]]sogs pa'i nges pa bshad par bya ste/_/bcom ldan 'das 'jam pa'i dbyangs ni zla ba nya ba'i mdangs can gyi sku la/_rdo rje dang /_rin po che dang /_pad+ma dang /_sna tshogs rdo rje'i phreng ba dang ldan pa'i cod pan gyi steng du/_sangs rgyas lnga'i rin po che'i rtse bran can du byas te lhag ma ni gsal bas 
[\[157b.3\]]go bar zad do/_/'khor los sgyur ba brgyad ni kun kyang gser gyi mdog can/_rdo rje'i skyil mo krung gis bzhugs pa/_rin po che'i dbu rgyan can/_phyag g.yas na 'khor lo bstod de bsnams pa/_phyag g.yon gyis gdan la mnyes pas brten par mdzad pa'o/_/zhe sdang chen po bcom ldan 'das mi 
[\[157b.4\]]bskyod pa ni/_/ut+pal sngon po'i mdog can zhal bzhi pa/_phyag brgyad pa rdo rje'i skyil mo krung gis glang po'i gdan la bzhugs pa/_rin po che'i dbu rgyan can/_zhal dang po khro bo sgeg pa/_zhal g.yas pa gdangs shing drag po/_zhal g.yon pa tshems kyi sgros gnon pa/_zhal rgyab ma mchog tu 
[\[157b.5\]]gdug pa thams cad skrag par mdzad pa/_phyag g.yas pa rnams na ral gri dang /_rdo rje dang /_mda' dang /_lcags kyu bsnams pa/_phyag g.yon rnams ni sdigs mdzub bsgreng ba dang /_dril bu dang /_gzhu dang zhags pa bsnams pa'o/_/chags pa chen po bcom ldan 'das rin chen 'byung ldan ni gser gyi mdog can rin po 
[\[157b.6\]]che'i dbu rgyan mnga' ba/_zhal ser po dang /_nag po dang /_dkar po dang /_dmar po dang ldan pa phyag brgyad pa/_rdo rje'i skyil mo krung gis rta'i gdan la bzhugs pa/_phyag g.yas rnams na rdo rje dang /_ral gri dang /_mda' dang /_lcags kyu bsnams pa/_phyag g.yon rnams na yid bzhin gyi nor bu'i rgyal mtshan 
[\[157b.7\]]dang /_dril bu dang /_gzhu dang zhags pa bsnams pa'o/_/'dod chags chen po bcom ldan 'das 'od dpag tu med pa ni sku mdog dmar po rin po che'i dbu rgyan can/_zhal bzhi pa/_phyag brgyad pa/_rdo rje'i skyil mo krung gis rma bya'i gdan la bzhugs pa/_zhal sgeg pa la sogs pa dang ldan pa dmar po dang nag po dang /_
[\[158a\]]
[\[158a.1\]]dkar po dang /_ser po mnga' ba/_phyag g.yas rnams ni rdo rje dang /_mda' dang /_ral gri dang /_lcags kyu bsnams pa/_phyag g.yon na pad+ma dang /_gzhu dang /_zhags pa dang /_dril bu bsnams pa'o/_/khro bo chen po bcom ldan 'das don yod grub pa ni sku mdog nag po rin po che'i dbu 
[\[158a.2\]]rgyan can/_zhal bzhi pa/_phyag brgyad pa/_rdo rje'i skyil mo krung gis nam mkha' lding gi khri la bzhugs pa/_zhal dang po mche ba gtsigs pa/_kha dog nag po g.yas pa zhi ba/_kha dog dkar po rgyab sgeg pa/_kha dog dmar po/_g.yon pa zhi ba'i tshul can mdog dkar po/_phyag g.yas pa rnams ral 
[\[158a.3\]]gri dang rdo rje dang /_mda' dang lcags kyu bsnams pa/_phyag g.yon rnams na dril bu dang /_gzhu dang sdigs mdzub bsgreng ba dang /_zhags pa bsnams pa'o/_/de bzhin gshegs pa de dag thams cad kyang nyi ma'i 'od mnga' ba/_rang gi gdan gyi steng du nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor la bzhugs par bya'o/_/rdo rje sems 
[\[158a.4\]]dpa' la sogs pa'ang sna tshogs rgyan dang gos 'chang ba/_dbang ldan phyogs nas brtsams nas ni/[ {]go rims [,]go rim [}]ji bzhin bri bar bya/_zhes bya ba la/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i dkyil 'khor de nyid du/_bcom ldan 'das mi bskyod pa'i dkyil 'khor la dbang ldan gyi phyogs su rdo rje sems dpa' sku mdog dkar po/_phyag 
[\[158a.5\]]g.yas kyis rang gi thugs kar rdo rje bstod pa'i tshul [(]kyis [,]gyis [)]bsnams pa/_g.yon bsnyems pas dril bu bsnams pa'o/_/pad+ma dang zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi steng na rdo rje'i skyil mo krung gis bzhugs pa/_rdo rje sems dpa'i gdan dang /_skyil mo krung dang /_dbu rgyan gyi nges pa ni rdo rje rgyal po la sogs pa 
[\[158a.6\]]dang /_thun mong yin la khyad par rnams ni bri bar bya ste/_rdo rje rgyal po sku mdog ser po rdo rje dang lcags kyu 'dren zhing bzhugs pa'o/_/rdo rje chags pa sku mdog dmar po mda' dang gzhu bsnams pa'o/_/rdo rje legs pa margad kyi mdog can phyag gnyis kyis legs so zhes bya ba stsol ba'o/_/bcom ldan 'das rin 
[\[158a.7\]]chen 'byung ldan gyi dkyil 'khor du/_rdo rje rin chen sku mdog ser po rang gi dbang bskur ba'i gnas su/_phyag gnyis kyis dbang bskur ba'i phreng ba 'ching zhing bzhugs pa'o/_/rdo rje nyi ma sku mdog dmar po/_phyag gnyis kyis rang gi thugs kar nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor bsnams pa'o/_/rdo rje 
[\[158b\]]
[\[158b.1\]]rgyal mtshan sku mdog nam mkha' ltar sngo bsangs/_phyag gnyis yid bzhin nor bu'i rgyal mtshan bsnams pa'o/_/rdo rje bzhad pa ni sku mdog pad+ma'i rtsa ltar dkar ba/_so'i phreng ba gnyis dang ldan pa'i rdo rje gnyis pa bsnams pa'o/_/bcom ldan 'das 'od dpag tu med pa'i dkyil 'khor du rdo rje chos 
[\[158b.2\]]sku mdog dmar skya/_phyag g.yon pa bsnyems pas pad+ma'i sdong bu 'dzin cing phyag g.yas pas pad+ma'i 'dab ma kha 'byed pa'o/_/rdo rje rnon po ni 'dir sku mdog gur gum lta bu phyag g.yas kyis ral gri 'phyar ba/_phyag g.yon pas thugs kar shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i glegs bam 
[\[158b.3\]]bsnams pa'o/_/rdo rje rgyu sku mdog gser gyi mdog can chos kyi 'khor lo'i phyag rgya dang ldan pa'o/_/rdo rje smra ba sku mdog dmar po phyag g.yas pa lce'i phyag dang ldan pa/_phyag g.yon pas dung bsnams pa'o/_/bcom ldan 'das don yod grub pa'i dkyil 'khor du/_rdo rje las sku mdog ljang 
[\[158b.4\]]gu phyag gnyis na rdo rje rtse gsum pa bsnams nas spyi bor 'jog cing de bzhin gshegs pa rnams la mchod pa'i don du gar mdzad cing bzhugs pa'o/_/rdo rje srung ba sku mdog ser po phyag gnyis kyis rdo rje go cha bsnams pa'o/_/rdo rje gnod sbyin sku mdog nag po phyag gnyis kyis mche ba gnyis rang gi zhal du 'dzin cing gdug pa thams 
[\[158b.5\]]cad 'jigs par mdzad pas bzhugs pa'o/_/rdo rje khu tshur sku mdog dkar po phyag gnyis kyis rdo rje khu tshur bsdams nas bzhugs pa'o/_/mtshams rnams su spyan ni 'jam pa'i dbyangs dang 'dra'o/_/mA ma kI ni mi bskyod pa dang 'dra'o/_/gos dkar mo ni 'od dpag tu med pa dang 'dra'o/_/sgrol ma ni don yod par grub pa dong 
[\[158b.6\]]'dra'o/_/sems ma bcu drug ni kun du bzang mo dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa'i lcags kyu mo dang /_dga' la chags ma dang legs ldan ma ste rdo rje rigs kyi'o/_/rin po che'i gtso mo dang /_rat+na'i sgrol ma dang /_rgyal mtshan rtse mo'i dpung rgyan dang /_bkod pa dang ldan ma dang bzhi ni rat+na'i rigs kyi'o/_/rdo rje 
[\[158b.7\]]sa mo dang kun du 'khor lo ma dang rlan 'dzin ma dang /_stong du zlos ma bzhi pad+mo rigs kyi'o/_/dngos grub kyi rtse mo dang /_thams cad bsrung ma dang /_mdangs slar 'phrog ma dang /_gzungs kyi phyag rgya rnams dang bzhi ni las kyi rigs kyi'o/_/sku mdog phyag mtshan sems dpa' bcu drug dang mthun no/_/
[\[159a\]]
[\[159a.1\]]'di'i lugs kyis spyan ma la rdo rje rigs kyi sems dpas bskor/_mA ma kI la rin po che'i rigs kyi sems dpa' bzhis bskor/_gzhan gnyis po yang rigs mthun pas bskor ba'o/_/'dir rdo rje thig gi nang logs su ri mo zur bzhi pa byas pa'i brgyad cha'i sgo ste rta 
[\[159a.2\]]babs med pa'o/_/lhag ma ni phyi'i dkyil 'khor ro/_/de'i shar gyi sgor rdo rje lcags kyu sku mdog dkar po rdo rje lcags kyu bsnams pa g.yas brkyang bas bzhugs pa'o/_/lho'i sgor rdo rje zhags pa sku mdog ser po phyag na rdo rje zhags pa bsnams pa g.yon brkyang bas bzhugs pa'o/_/nub kyi sgor 
[\[159a.3\]]rdo rje lcags sgrog sku mdog dmar po phyag gnyis kyis rdo rje lu gu rgyud bsnams pa sa ga'i 'dug stangs kyis bzhugs pa'o/_/byang phyogs kyi sgor rdo rje 'bebs pa sku mdog ljang gu phyag gnyis kyis rdo rje bsdams pas rdo rje dang dril bu bsnams pa zlum po'i 'dug stangs kyis bzhugs par bri bar bya ste nang gi dkyil 'khor ro/_/
[\[159a.4\]]gnyis pa nang gi dkyil 'khor gyi nyis 'gyur te gzungs kyi dkyil 'khor ro/_/gnyis pa der yang shar phyogs su sa bcu gnyis bri bar bya ste/_kun kyang sna tshogs pad+ma la sems dpa'i skyil mo krung gis bzhugs pa/_rin po che'i dbu rgyan can/_phyag g.yas na rdo rje bsnams pa/_phyag g.yon na rang gi mtshan ma 
[\[159a.5\]]bsnams pa'o/_/de la mos pas spyod pa'i sa ni sku mdog pad+ma ltar dmar ba phyag g.yon na pad+ma dmar po bsnams pa'o/_/rab tu dga' ba'i sa ni rin po che pad+ma rA ga'i mdog can/_phyag g.yon na yid bzhin gyi nor bu bsnams pa'o/_/dri ma med pa'i sa ni nor bu tshon shel gyi mdog can/_phyag g.yon gyis thams cad 
[\[159a.6\]]dag par so sor rtog pa'i pad+ma dkar po bsnams pa'o/_/'od byed pa'i sa ni nyi ma 'char ka'i mdog can/_phyag g.yon gyis sna tshogs pad+ma'i steng du nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor bsnams pa'o/_/'od 'phro ba'i sa ni margad lta bu'i mdog can phyag g.yon na ut+pal bsnams pa'o/_/shin tu sbyangs 
[\[159a.7\]]dka' ba'i sa ni sku mdog ser po phyag g.yon pa pang bar bkan nas bzhag pas nor bu markata bsnams pa'o/_/mngon du gyur pa'i sa ni gser btso ma lta bu'i mdog can/_phyag g.yon gyis pad+ma'i steng du shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i glegs bam bsnams pa'o/_/ring du song ba'i 
[\[159b\]]
[\[159b.1\]]sa ni nam mkha' ltar sngo bsangs phyag g.yon gyis sna tshogs pad+ma'i steng du sna tshogs rdo rje bsnams pa'o/_/mi g.yo ba'i sa ni ston ka'i zla ba ltar dkar ba/_phyag g.yon pa bsnyems pas pad+ma'i steng du zla ba'i dkyil 'khor de'i steng du rdo rje lnga pa mdog dmar po ste/_pad+ma'i sdong bu 'dzin pa'o/_/
[\[159b.2\]]legs pa'i blo gros kyi ni sku mdog dmar skya phyag g.yon gyis ut+pa la'i steng du ral gri bsnams pa'o/_/chos kyi sprin gyi ni gser gyi mdog can phyag g.yon gyis chos kyi sprin gyis yongs su bskor ba'i shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i glegs bam bsnams pa'o/_/sangs rgyas kyi kun du 'od ni nyi ma 
[\[159b.3\]]gung gi mdog can phyag g.yon pas pad+ma'i steng du yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas ston par byed pa'i sangs rgyas kyi sku gzugs bsnams pa'o/_/lho phyogs su pha rol tu phyin pa bcu gnyis sna tshogs pad+ma'i steng du zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la sems dpa'i skyil mo krung gis bzhugs pa/_rgyan sna tshogs 'chang 
[\[159b.4\]]ba/_gzhon zhing dar la bab pa zhal gyi pad+ma sgeg pa'i nyams kyis rgyas pa/_rin po che sna tshogs pas spras pa'i gser gyi gdu bus lag ngar bkang ba/_rin po che'i rgyan dang /_rtse bran mnga' ba/_phyag g.yas pas sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa yongs su rdzogs par mdzad pa'i phyir yid 
[\[159b.5\]]bzhin gyi nor bu rin po che bsnams pa/_phyag g.yon pas rang gi mtshan ma bsnams pa rim bzhin du bri bar bya'o/_/de la rin po che pad+ma'i pha rol tu phyin pa ni pad+ma dmar po ltar sku mdog dmar po phyag g.yon pas pad+ma'i steng du zla ba'i dkyil 'khor bsnams pa'o/_/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa sku 
[\[159b.6\]]mdog dmar skya/_phyag g.yon pas 'bru dang rin po che sna tshogs pa'i snye ma bsnams pa'o/_/tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa sku mdog dkar po phyag g.yon pas a shwa ka'i me tog gi mgo lcogs 'dab ma dang bcas pa bsnams pa'o/_/bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa gser gyi mdog can phyag 
[\[159b.7\]]g.yon pas pad+ma dkar po bsnams pa'o/_/brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa markata kyi mdog can/_phyag g.yon pas ut+pa la bsnams pa'o/_/bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa nam mkha' ltar sngo bsangs phyag g.yon pas pad+ma dkar po bsnams pa'o/_/shes rab kyi pha rol 
[\[160a\]]
[\[160a.1\]]tu phyin pa gser gyi mdog can phyag g.yas kyis chos kyi 'khor lo'i phyag rgya dang ldan pa'o/_/phyag g.yon gyis shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i glegs bam bsnams pa'o/_/thabs kyi pha rol tu phyin pa pri yang ku ltar sku mdog ljang gu phyag g.yon pas pad+ma dkar 
[\[160a.2\]]po'i steng du rdo rje bsnams pa'o/_/smon lam gyi pha rol tu phyin pa ut+pa la sngon po lta bu'i mdog can phyag g.yon gyis ut+pa la'i steng du ral gri bsnams pa'o/_/stobs kyi pha rol tu phyin pa nyi ma'i mdog can phyag g.yon gyis shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i glegs bam bsnams pa'o/_/ye shes 
[\[160a.3\]]kyi pha rol tu phyin pa ston ka'i zla ba lta bu'i mdog can phyag g.yon pas rin po che sna tshogs kyi 'bras bus brgyan pa'i byang chub kyi shing gi yal ga bsnams pa'o/_/rdo rje las kyi pha rol tu phyin pa sku mdog sna tshogs pa phyag g.yon pas sna tshogs pad+ma bsnams pa'o/_/nub phyogs su dbang bcu pad+ma dang 
[\[160a.4\]]zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi steng du sems dpa'i skyil mo krung gis bzhugs pa/_rgyan dang na bza' sna tshogs pa mnga' ba/_gzhon zhing dar la bab pa/_sgeg pa'i nyams dang ldan pa/_zhal gyi pad+ma cung zad 'dzum pa dang /_spyan ut+pa la sngon po'i 'dab ma 'dra ba/_'od mtha' yas pa'i rin po 
[\[160a.5\]]che'i dbu rgyan can/_phyag g.yas na pad+ma bsnams pa/_g.yon na bsnyems pas rang gi mtshan ma bsnams par rim bzhin du bri bar bya/_/de la tshe la dbang ba sku mdog dmar skya/_phyag g.yon na pad+ma rA ga'i nor bu'i 'dab ma'i dbus su bcom ldan 'das tshe dpag tu med pa ting nge 'dzin 
[\[160a.6\]]la snyoms par bzhugs pa'i sku bsnams pa'o/_/sems la dbang ba zla ba'i mdog can phyag g.yon gyis me tog ban+du ka ltar dmar ba'i rdo rje rtse lnga pa bsnams pa'o/_/yo byad la dbang ba sku mdog ser po phyag g.yon na yid bzhin gyi nor bu rin po che'i rgyal mtshan bsnams pa'o/_/las la 
[\[160a.7\]]dbang ba markata lta bu'i mdog can phyag g.yon sna tshogs rdo rje bsnams pa'o/_/skye ba la dbang ba sku mdog sna tshogs pa/_phyag g.yon kha dog sna tshogs pa dzA ti'i yal ga bsnams pa'o/_/rdzu 'phrul la dbang ba sku mdog ljang gu phyag g.yon na pad+ma'i steng du nyi ma dang zla ba'i dkyil 
[\[160b\]]
[\[160b.1\]]'khor bsnams pa'o/_/mos pa la dbang ba pad+ma'i rtsa ba ltar mdog dkar ba/_phyag g.yon na me tog pri yang ku'i snye ma bsnams pa'o/_/smon lam la dbang ba gser gyi mdog can phyag g.yon na ut+pa la sngon po bsnams pa'o/_/ye shes la dbang ba mthon ka chen po'i mdog can/_phyag g.yon na ut+pa la sngon

[\[186a.5\]]ba'i lag pa la mthe bos dbus su bzhag pa ni me lha'i 'o/_/mdzub mo dang mthe bo sbyar nas dbyig par byas pa ni gshin rje'i 'o/_/de nyid las 'khor lo lta bur byas nas gzhag pa ni khyab 'jug gi phyag rgya'o/_/lag pa gcig na thod pa thogs pa lta bu ni ma mo rnams kyi phyag rgya'o/_/
